# State of the Build — Sailor

*Surveyed 2026-07-25 (three parallel codebase sweeps). Update when surfaces land or capabilities change. Companion to [VISION.md](VISION.md) and [ROADMAP.md](ROADMAP.md).*

## Scale

~158k lines in `frontend/app` · 308 components · 27 registered node types · ~22 creative surfaces · 389 unit specs + 20 E2E specs · `frontend/server` 8.6k lines · 50 house styles · 50 image models + ~17 video models.

### Frame Relight — live lights as a layer effect, stage 1 — LANDED 2026-09-30 (spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-30-relight-layer-effect-design.md`, plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-30-relight-stage1.md`, mockup artifact 7rAD2Mu5a2S5d34kHU42iy, prototype `frontend/app/pages/dev/relight.vue` (uncommitted); `a44abbda0`..`318971906`, non-contiguous — 9 build tasks, subagent-driven, a review per task, a whole-feature review, its fix wave and a re-review)

From "are there better relighting systems than ours" (Photoshop's Relight beta, Focal's instant demo). Julien chose: a layer EFFECT, the prototype's sliders and presets, Nano Banana 2 for the later Finish, the old Relight node parked.

**What changed.** A pinned GPU effect `relight` on image (and wired) layers, right after `dof` in the stack, under Light & shadow in the + menu, plus right-click an image → **Relight…**. Selecting its row shows up to three glowing light dots on the layer (drag = one undo step, scroll = height with one undo step per gesture, double-click the layer = add a light, dashed ring = behind) and dims the rest of the Frame. Panel: Setups (Window, Golden key, Rim, Neon, Under), light chips, per light colour swatches / Brightness / Height / Reach / on, then Original light, Depth, Texture, Shine, Shadows, and a hold-to-Compare. Local depth only — no paid calls.

**How.** `lib/relight/`: `settings.ts` + `presets.ts`; `depthFieldCore.ts` (pure: float conversion, edge-preserving smoothing, photo-guided joint upsampling, GL row order, a 64 MB LRU cache pinned per paint) and `depthField.ts` + `depthFieldWorker.ts` (the field is built from the layer's SOURCE image at native size ≤1536 px, in a Web Worker, drawn plain until ready, then a repaint); `relightPass.ts` (the prototype's shader on `GpuPost`: top-down lighting space, `uDepthRect` maps the layer's crop onto the whole-image field, contact shadows only). `GpuPost` takes an R16F float field and keeps it uploaded between paints. `useCompositorLayers.ts` runs relight then dof in one offscreen step (`gpuContent`); a document without Relight renders as before.

**Proven.** Unit tests (settings, field maths incl. the 8-bit-stripe, halo and stair-step cases, crop rect, cache, pass packing, panel, gesture undo); `tests/relight-effect.spec.ts` 7/7 in the real editor with real mouse input, asserting the GPU run counter (not only pixels): right-click add, drag + one undo, setups, Compare, light-at-top brightens the top half, wheel gesture, first-add stall (longest main-thread block 35–38 ms, lit ~3 s later; was 1.4–4.3 s before the worker). Neon matches the prototype by eye, including on a 16:9 cover-cropped layer.

**Caught in review.** A dof-only layer at aperture 0 stopped prefetching depth; the field was first built synchronously inside paints (seconds, also on cards); the field cache ignored size and crop, and a not-yet-decoded photo could poison it; cover-cropped layers were lit off-register; Relight was offered on text and shapes; scrolling over a light also panned the canvas and every wheel tick was an undo snapshot; Relight swallowed double-click everywhere; the depth field was re-uploaded every paint; Compare could stick.

**Open.** Stage 4 (Frame light link + Motion dials), node (parked). Web export shows a "left out" notice instead of pre-rendering. Wired SLOTS have the plumbing but no UI to add Relight (same as dof). Handles ignore skew / corner-pin / warp. One worker job error sends the rest of the session to the synchronous fallback. The breadcrumb tooltip (`EFFECT_HINTS.relight`) lives only in another session's uncommitted edit to CompositorModal.vue. Hands-on trackpad pass owed.

**Stage 2 — MoGe-2 surfaces — LANDED 2026-09-30** (plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-30-relight-stage2.md`; `11e7e44af`..`f46078fdb`, non-contiguous — 5 tasks, subagent-driven, a review per task, a light-direction debug pass, a whole-stage review, its fix wave and a re-review). When a photo gets Relight in the open Frame editor, `POST /api/depth/surfaces` reads its surfaces once with `fal-ai/moge-2` (vitl-normal, 3D exports off, photo sent at ≤1536 px) through the metered `runFal`, and caches `input/sailor_depth/moge_<hash>.png` (hash of the original bytes). Price: one constant in `shared/pricing/relightSurfaces.ts`, $0.0125 → 3 credits, confidence estimate. The Relight panel shows `Reading shape · ~$0.01` (hosted: `3 credits`) while a paid read is in flight; the effect lights from local depth meanwhile and swaps to the model's surfaces when they land (`GpuPost` gains one extra texture; the shader decodes red = right, green = up, blue = toward camera, through the same crop mapping as depth). Paints never pay: only the editor requests, via a cheap signature watch over visible Relight layers. Server: kill switch `NUXT_RELIGHT_SURFACES=off` (503 {off:true}), rate limit, hosted ownership (inputs; outputs through the /view gate incl. the harvest retry, keyed on the path actually read; temp as /view), in-flight dedupe (two tabs pay once), atomic cache write, 64 MB capped download, 600 s poll then "still reading", 402/unpriced refusals quiet. The free depth route got the same ownership gate. Browser tests mock the route everywhere. **Paid check:** one call on `ComfyUI_00084_.png` — 204 s (cold start) then a free cache hit; fal's usage API refused the key (403), so the price stays an estimate. **Caught:** a failed read re-fired a paid call on every edit; hosted wired layers lost their free depth; browser tests could make real paid calls on any machine without the gitignored cache; two tabs could pay twice; a deep watch walked every layer per drag; an empty placeholder texture spammed WebGL warnings; hidden layers paid for unused surfaces; the light-direction test was fooled by the puppy's brighter floor (now judged by per-half gain, with and without surfaces, plus a decode check with a negative control). **Open:** a read past 600 s is abandoned, not cancelled (fal may bill it; Sailor absorbs in hosted); cold-wait billing unverified; the price shows only for the selected effect's photo; hidden GROUPS still request once; two surfaced photos in one frame alternate re-uploads; the rate limit is per socket IP (shared behind a proxy).

**Read shape button — LANDED 2026-09-30** (`609fdb626`..`50786475c`, non-contiguous). Julien asked for the paid read to be a click, not automatic. The editor now only PEEKS (`peek:true` → cached answer or `{absent:true}`, never calls fal, own 120/min bucket); a photo with no cached surfaces shows **Read shape · ~$0.01** (hosted `3 credits`, tooltip explains) in the Relight panel, and only that click pays. A cached photo gets its surfaces with no button. A failed peek still shows the button (the click answers from cache when there is one). Browser tests: new photo → zero reads until the click, then exactly one; cached photo → peek only.

**Stage 3 — Finish with Nano Banana 2 — LANDED 2026-09-30** (plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-30-relight-stage3-finish.md`; `e2963c125`..`309b38c67`, non-contiguous — 4 tasks, subagent-driven, a review per task, a whole-stage review, its fix wave). The Relight panel shows **Finish · ~$0.08** (hosted `16 credits`, tooltip "about 20 s") on a still photo — not a cut-out, not a looping clip, WebGL2 present, not while Edit image runs. A click renders a pixel-aligned pair from the live paint's own maths (`renderRelightPair`: the layer's box at the SOURCE's resolution ≤1536 px, untinted; original and lit guide), sends both to `POST /api/inpaint/relight-finish` (`fal-ai/nano-banana-2/edit`, 1K, fixed server-side prompt in `shared/pricing/relightFinish.ts`, metered `runFal`, kill switch `NUXT_RELIGHT_FINISH=off`, data:image only ≤20 MB, 502 on provider failure), and lands the result in the Undo / Try again / Keep bar. Applying is ONE `setLocal`: new photo, cover crop, Relight row removed (so the result is never relit nor sent for a paid surfaces read); Undo restores photo, crop and stack; Try again re-sends the same pair; the bar clears itself on undo, another layer or a deleted layer; the panel is locked during the call; one call per click; hosted cost confirm on Finish and Try again. **Price:** `nano-banana-2/edit` corrected to fal's verified $0.08 / 16 credits (Pose too). **Prompt revised after the paid check:** shadows only on surfaces already in the picture, flat backgrounds stay flat, illustrations stay illustrations (the bake-off wording invented a wall behind a flat illustration). **Paid checks (5 calls, ~$0.44):** puppy Golden key → real floor shadow, identity kept (one of three puppy draws lit from the wrong side — Try again exists for that); flat illustration Neon → background kept flat under the new prompt; ~12–20 s per call. **Caught:** provider failures were a bare 500; tint baked twice; stretch instead of crop; a clip hid the result; preview-sized results; stretched layers sent cropped; Finish and Edit image could run together (two paid calls, one lost); undo-then-Try-again wrote under the old crop and Relight; cut-outs paid for a re-cut that deleted the shadows; and, pre-existing, **the Undo / Try again / Keep bar sat under the prompt dock for a full-bleed layer** (Edit image too) — the dock now steps aside while the bar is up. **Open:** a photo with any transparency counts as a cut-out (no Finish); deselecting on empty canvas doesn't clear the bar; Texture relief samples finer at source resolution than on screen; the model sometimes ignores the guide's direction (Try again).

### Frame Motion — Pixel reveal transition — LANDED 2026-09-28 (plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-28-frame-pixel-reveal.md`, prototype `docs/superpowers/specs/assets/2026-09-28-pixel-reveal-prototype.html`; `edf447b01`..`6b27a7f69`, non-contiguous — 4 build tasks, subagent-driven, a review per task, a whole-feature review, its fix wave and a re-review)

From pixel-text-reveal.vercel.app (Julien: "i'd like to build it as a motion effect", "any layer", then on the prototype "this is awesome. please implement"). The site ships without a licence: its JS was read to understand the technique; every line here is our own.

**What changed.** A new Motion-tab transition, behaviour kind `pixelreveal`, on the existing `reveal` track (like Dither/Settle): ANY layer starts as coarse blocks, each the exact mean colour of the layer under it, blocks split in four at jittered thresholds (Levels) and the layer turns sharp; when each block goes = a direction mixed with a pattern (random, clusters, scanlines, rain, typewriter, zigzag, cascade, ordered dither, flow); fresh blocks arrive in a heat colour and cool. Nine looks × In/Out = 18 gallery tiles (Materialize, Signal, Typewriter, Dissolve, Rain, Radiate, Bitmap, Glitch, Flow — the prototype's numbers). Text splits into words / letters / lines that can rise out of their line; everything else is one piece. Inspector: Look, In/Out, Pieces, Pattern, Direction, Pixel, Levels, Front width, Heat. A look's own choreography is scaled to fill the bar. Pixel is in frame px at 1080 wide, so exports match the editor.

**How.** `lib/motionx/reveal/pixelReveal.ts` (DOM-free: looks, the one reader, `pickGrid`, piece timing, CPU mirrors for the tiles); `paintPixelReveal.ts` — its own lazily created WebGL2 context: the layer drawn alone (`soloPass`), pieces packed into a grid-aligned power-of-two atlas, `generateMipmap` (the first use in the app) so a block reads its exact mean at mip k (grid m·2^k, m∈{1,3}), one draw per piece, `drawImage`d back and stamped with the layer's opacity and blend. Text pieces come from `textMotionCells` + `groupCells` (advance boxes, clipped to their own tile when rising). End of the bar is exact: the fold hands the painter nothing at amount 1.

**Proven.** ~110 unit tests (core, fold/labels/tiles, painter seams, inspector); `tests/motion-pixel-reveal.spec.ts` in the real editor (a Materialize bar from the gallery: hot heat-colour blocks mid-bar, 0 differing px at the end vs the plain layer); a live contact sheet of per-word rise and refinement sent to Julien.

**Caught in review.** Rising pieces clipped accented capitals/ascenders to the line pitch, then popped (fixed: a piece's band is its own tile). Exports never warmed the outline font, so an export could be whole-layer or mix whole and per-word frames — the embed plan, the bake and the editor now warm it like Morph. The GL context latched off for the session on one refused `getContext`, yet never latched real failures (context churn) — now compile/link latches, everything else backs off 0.5→30 s. Shadows/glows on split text were cut — a text layer with a visible effect now reveals as one piece. A reveal bar no longer offers "Open into keyframes".

**Open.** Performance at 4K with several revealing layers (atlas re-uploaded + mipmapped per layer per frame, ~250 MB held after a 4K export, a `gl.finish` per layer) — measure before tuning. Pulse pivot on a single-copy cloner offset; Pieces lacks `aria-disabled`; tile block size hand-picked; the embed plan warms fonts for bars on text that will not split (harmless). `tests/unit/embed-build-output` fails on `frame-lean.js` size (459,833 > 430,000) — PRE-EXISTING (bundle built 09-27, contains no pixel-reveal code); the painter will add weight at the next embed rebuild.

### Face models off non-commercial weights — Face Swap on Easel, Person swap (video) on Pixverse, Fix faces on Topaz — BUILT 2026-09-27, families OFF (spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-26-non-commercial-face-models-replacement-design.md`, plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-27-non-commercial-face-models-replacement.md`; `e8fef3f9d`..`0ca0adfeb`, non-contiguous — 5 tasks, subagent-driven, a review per task, a whole-branch review and one fix wave)

InsightFace (buffalo_l, inswapper), CodeFormer and Wav2Lip are licensed for non-commercial use only, and three shipped features used them. **Face Swap** (`FaceSwap`) now runs on fal's Easel advanced face swap (family `face-swap`, $0.05 a picture). The node gained Gender ("Not chosen"/"Male"/"Female"/"Non-binary", refused before the hold while unchosen) and Keep hair from ("The picture"/"The face photo"); the old `face_index`/`threshold` and the `swap_mask` output are gone, and old saved nodes are converted on load (`app/lib/graph/migrateFaceNodes.ts`). **Person swap (video)** (`PersonSwapVideo`, new, canvas only) runs on fal's Pixverse Swap in person mode (family `person-swap-video`). The video must be uploaded to Sailor and is measured before the hold (MP4/MOV/WebM, ≤ 100 MB, ≤ 10 s); the price is $0.15/$0.15/$0.20 at 360p/540p/720p, doubled over 5 s. **Fix faces** (`FixFacesNode`) runs on fal's Topaz image upscale, Standard V2 with face enhancement (family `fix-faces`), with Strength 0.8, Creativity 0 and Upscale 2× by default. It is priced by output size ($0.08 ≤ 24 MP … $1.36 ≤ 512 MP), with the input capped at the shared ~19 MP. **Removed:** the local `FaceRestore` and `LipSync` (Wav2Lip) nodes, `CodeformerRemoteNode`, the `faceswap`/`facerestore`/`lipsync` model bundles and the InsightFace auto-download; the Lip-sync studio (`LipSyncNode`) is untouched. The Python nodes are definition-only and fail plainly on ComfyUI; the three nodes now sit under `api node/…/fal`. **The Face Swap app** is the first mini app on the runner. It needs `cards` + `face-swap`, and its result wait listens before the POST resolves and buffers early events (`app/lib/runner/awaitRunnerResult.ts`). The `/object_info` baseline was regenerated, and `scripts/snapshot_object_info.mjs` now passes jiti the `#shared` alias; it had stopped loading.

**Verified:** per-family runner specs (payloads against saved fal schemas, prices per tier and boundary, refusals, engine end-to-end hold = charge), phase-b e2e FLOWS for all three, the app spec (early event, rejected start), the load migration, and `native-small-routes-parity` run for real against the Python engine. Typecheck unchanged at 438. No paid call has been made.

**Owed:** one live call per family (~$0.50) before switching on; the Face Swap app by hand. **Known limits:** the app skips the canvas price-confirm step; already-downloaded inswapper/CodeFormer/Wav2Lip files stay on disk and the Models manager can't list them; no edit-model A/B for face swap yet.

### Frame brush — steadying and hold to snap — LANDED 2026-09-26 (spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-26-frame-brush-steadying-design.md`, plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-26-frame-brush-steadying.md`, prototype `docs/superpowers/specs/assets/2026-09-26-brush-steady-prototype.html`; `26d91a970`..`78e2b07a9`, non-contiguous — 5 tasks, subagent-driven, a review per task, a whole-feature review, its fix wave and a re-review)

From Procreate, for a trackpad hand. **Steadying** runs before any tip paints (spray can, round, bristle; Paint and Effect mode; the eraser too): **Streamline** (the brush trails your finger and catches up; on release it finishes the line to where you stopped, ≤ 350 ms), **Stabilisation** (recency-weighted average) and **Motion filtering** (a One Euro filter). **Hold to snap** (Round and Bristle): stop at the end and keep holding (a small ring fills, 0.63 s by default) and the stroke becomes a **Line**, **Arc**, **Ellipse/Circle** or a **Shape** with real corners; keep holding and move to adjust (a line's end follows, an ellipse turns and resizes), **Shift** gives a circle or a 15° line; a stroke that isn't a clean shape stays as drawn; the spray can never snaps (holding pools and drips). Controls: a **Steadying** section in the brush inspector (Streamline 30%, Stabilisation 15%, Motion filtering 40%, Hold to snap on, Hold time 0.63 s, Reset), explanations as tooltips; one set for all tips, persisted. A stroke saves the steadied / snapped path — the `TipStroke` format is unchanged and replay stays exact. Pure modules `lib/brushTips/steady.ts` and `lib/brushTips/quickShape.ts`; `useBrushPaint.replaceTipSamples` swaps in a fresh record (the per-stroke box cache assumes append-only).

**Verified:** unit suites (295 across brush/tip/steady/quickshape); real-mouse E2E on the running app (`tests/compositor-brush-steady.spec.ts`: a jittery zig-zag ~10× calmer with steadying on, a held line snaps dead straight, a loop → Ellipse → Circle with Shift, dragging while held grows it 0.109 → 0.164, one undo removes it, reload identical, the spray can never snaps, settings persist). Parts 1–3 E2E still green (the painted-effect test failed once inside an 8-minute batch and passed 3/3 alone).

**Known limits.** A symmetric kidney/bean snaps to an ellipse (the ellipse test runs first); no editing a snapped shape after release; one set of settings for all tips; the inspector's controls are hand-built like the tip settings, not the shared Studio components. Not yet tried by Julien on his trackpad.

### Frame layout grid — stages 1, 2 and 4 LANDED; stage 3 PARKED — 2026-09-27

Spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-26-frame-layout-grid-design.md`, plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-26-frame-layout-grid.md`, tasks 1–7. One standard layout grid per Frame (`lib/frame/layoutGrid.ts`): columns (count, gutter, margin, Stretch/Center/Left) and rows (Off/Square/Count) on a baseline grid of half the body text's line spacing, migrated from the old `sailor_localGrid` and defaulting to the format's suggested grid. The editor (`layoutGrid` / `layoutGridResolved` / `setLayoutGrid` / `ensureLayoutGrid` / `dragMoving` / `gridSnapLines`) drives a DOM overlay (`LayoutGridOverlay.vue`) — faint column lines always, modules + baselines fading in only while a layer is moving past 4 px, an accent fill on what it covers — and a "Layout grid" panel (`LayoutGridSection.vue`) in the Frame's properties. ⌃G (Mac) / Ctrl+Shift+4 (elsewhere) toggles it; box moves and resizes snap to it; every change is one undo step. A Frame that already has layers defaults the grid to hidden, so nothing changes for existing Frames until it's turned on.

**Verified (stage 1):** real-mouse Playwright proof against the running `:3002` (`frontend/tests/frame-layout-grid.spec.ts`, 5/5 green): the overlay shows column lines with no leftover debug colour; clicking a layer selects it without fading in the modules or recording a history step; a real mouse drag fades the modules in past the 4 px slop and lands a moved layer's edge within 0.5 px of a resolved grid line (checked against `resolveLayoutGrid(...).xs`, not asserted in the abstract); ⌃G hides the overlay and ⌘Z restores it; changing the column count is one undo step. `/dev/frame-lab`'s `window.__frameLab` gained `editor` / `layoutGrid` / `layoutGridResolved` getters (via a `defineExpose` on `CompositorModal`) so the test can read history revisions and the grid's own snap lines instead of inferring them from pixels — dev-only, nothing in the app reads it.

Stage 2 (plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-27-frame-layout-grid-stage2.md`): text snaps by what is drawn. `lib/frame/textMetrics.ts` measures capitals and baselines exactly as `drawText` lays lines out. While the grid shows, a move puts the capitals on a row top or the last baseline on a row bottom, else the first baseline on the baseline grid (`lib/frame/gridSnap.ts`). Re-snap does the same, and a box with nothing in reach rounds its top to the baseline grid. Selected text shows a dashed accent line at its capitals and solid ones at its baselines; a drag shows a badge ("4 × 3 modules" / "4 columns"). The Transform section has Column / Span and Row / Span (`LayerGridFields.vue`; `spanOf`/`placeOnSpan` in `layoutGrid.ts`, reused by stage 4). The text box's "col" unit reads whole columns. Stages 3 (Layout tab on the grid) and 4 (responsive spans, agent) are owed; retiring `lib/frame/gridConfig.ts` and the `FrameGrid` alias waits on them.

**Verified (stage 2):** the five stage-2 unit specs (text metrics, grid snap, layout grid, editor, UI) green — 99/99 at the end of the tasks, more after the final fix wave; two new real-mouse Playwright tests on `:3002` (dragging text lands its capitals on a row top with a badge on the way; typing a Column moves the layer), 11/11 with stage 1's; one transient failure of stage 1's first test on a cold compile, green alone and on the next full run. The final fix wave gave disabled Grid fields their reasons as tooltips, kept a typed Column/Row inside the grid, and made text re-measure when a web font loads.


Stage 4 (plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-27-frame-layout-grid-stage4.md`, tasks 1–4): responsive Frames hold to grid spans — `lib/frame/responsive/spans.ts` (`gridsAt`, `holdRows`, `holdOf`) replaces `sections.ts`; a layer on columns (and rows) keeps them at other sizes, one touching a frame edge holds to the frame; the pins card reads "Holds to: Grid / Whole frame" through the same `holdOf` the resolver uses. The overlay draws one path per layer (baselines under 3 px and > 2,000 modules left out) and, at a viewing size, the resolver's own grid; Grid fields and Re-snap return to the design size first. Dropping at a viewing size re-holds the layer so it draws where it was dropped. **Verified:** 310 unit tests, 14/14 real-mouse Playwright on `:3002`. **Open:** a drop exactly on a row's tolerance edge can still jump on release (rare); on coarse grids (2 columns) at wide viewing sizes a drop can land up to 250 px from the pointer — both with known fixes, parked for the owner. **Task 5 (removing `gridConfig.ts`, the `FrameGrid` alias) waits for stage 3.**

Stage 3 (plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-27-frame-layout-grid-stage3.md`) is PARKED at its switch. Tasks 1–7 are committed and inert (the sheet can take the grid — columns, band, rows, type from the Line — and the checker has rules 11–12 plus a settle; no product path passes the grid yet). The switch that makes the Layout tab plan on the visible grid is saved as `.superpowers/sdd/2026-09-27-frame-layout-grid-stage3/task-8-wip2.patch`: with it, layout options fall 5% (7,203 → 6,838), one layout under half, none at zero, 85 tests failing — and it needs owner calls that change the spec (Line in whole px, rows only with ≥ 8, Pinterest's grid left-aligned, settle reach). Until then the Layout tab's layouts don't line up with the visible columns. The suggested Line already changed (a51fa1f97: sized so body capitals are one unit and clear format legibility floors), so auto grids' unit/margins/rows are 10–30% larger than stage 1 shipped.

### Frame brush — more shaders and painted effects — LANDED 2026-09-26 (Part 3 of "painting with shaders"; spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-26-frame-brush-shaders-effects-design.md`, plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-26-frame-brush-shaders-effects.md`; `ce1f49ec3`..`85786e4f9` — 6 tasks, subagent-driven, a review per task, a whole-feature review, its fix wave and a re-review)

Two additions to the brush. **More…** in the Paint row opens the shader library (generative shaders + the Material category, My effects first); the chosen shader becomes an extra swatch and paints a brush layer whose fill is that shader, fixed to the surface (`anchor: 'frame'`) for every tip. A third mode, **Effect** (Paint · Effect · Mask), paints where an effect changes **everything beneath**: Ripple, Reeded glass, Glow, Dither, Colour split, Pixels, Frost, or More… (any shader that reads its input). An effect layer is an ordinary brush layer with `showPaint: false` and one `backdrop_shader` (speed 0): its paint never draws, but its coverage is the effect's mask and strength — a spray pass is light, a second pass strengthens it, the eraser weakens it, Opacity fades it. The panel shows "Painted effect · <name>" (explanation in the tooltip) and **Change effect…**; the effect's dials live in its effect row as for any effect. Mode, effect and chosen shader are remembered. Hidden-paint layers stay clickable where painted; exports never ship the paint; the layers-list icon shows the paint shape.

**Verified:** unit suites; real-mouse E2E on the running app (`tests/compositor-brush-effects.spec.ts`: Pixels over half a word changes that half by 17–19 and the other half by 0, the flat band under the paint by 0; a second pass 36.5; the eraser 23.8; reload redraws identical pixels; More… paints a library shader; toolbar 401×147 at 1024 wide); Parts 1–2 E2E still green (10/10).

**Found on the way.** Reading the stage canvas directly (`toDataURL`/`getImageData`) repeatedly makes Chromium move it off the GPU, after which edges anti-alias slightly differently — reloads looked non-identical. `stackPixels` now reads a copy; new `stackImageData` helper (`8268505fc`). The three-mode toolbar first wrapped "Mask" onto its own line (a 182 px bar covering the Frame); the bar may now grow to 460 px wide.

**Known limits.** Library shaders don't follow the stroke (only the six materials do); one effect per brush layer from the toolbar; an effect always applies to everything beneath, not to one chosen layer. Not yet tried by Julien with a trackpad.

### Frame brush materials — paint with foil, chrome, lava, ink, neon, oil — LANDED 2026-09-26 (Part 2 of "painting with shaders"; spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-26-frame-brush-materials-design.md`, plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-26-frame-brush-materials.md`; `65eff8928`..`cfd7cc130`, non-contiguous — 7 tasks, subagent-driven, a review per task, a whole-feature review, its fix wave and a re-review)

The brush toolbar gains a **Paint** row: Colour (as before) or six live materials — Holographic foil, Liquid chrome, Lava, Marbled ink, Neon, Oil slick. **Round and Bristle strokes carry the material along the line** (each stroke is its own object — chrome's highlight runs down the stroke); **the spray can's material sits on the surface**. A brush layer stores `material {id, moving}` (not a fill type — other surfaces never see it); a new layer starts when the toolbar paint differs from the selected layer; the layer panel has Material and a **Moving** switch (off = still and free: no clock, cached). The GPU engine writes stroke coordinates per group (RGBA32F) and shades the material there; output is valid premultiplied colour; neon glows 16 Frame units past the paint (render-only pad, also in corner pin / silhouette / DOF). Unknown material ids render as Colour. Colour-only and legacy brush layers render byte-identically.

**Verified:** unit suites; headless GPU compile + render of every material × tip; real-mouse E2E on the running app (`tests/compositor-brush-materials.spec.ts`: a layer per material, Moving off identical after 1 s, reload identical, Moving on changes, the same coverage at two render sizes — 8.41% vs 8.26%).

**Known limits.** Neon's glow changes hue just past the paint edge; neon's core reads white-hot; a neon brush with depth of field has a slightly stretched depth map; the stroke-coordinate texture can reach 1 GiB at the largest export size (an out-of-memory drops to surface coordinates for the session). Not yet tried by Julien with a trackpad.

**Next.** Part 3: "More shaders" from the full library and painted effects.

### The pen — Trim joins the pieces it leaves (auto-join) — LANDED 2026-09-27

Commit 6e5d0d519. On the owner's flower ("why doesn't the shape close inside"): Trim left every remaining piece as its own open path even where two now ended at the same point, so the Frame closed each piece with a chord instead of filling the centre. Now, at the trim's own end points only, two open paths of the same kind ending at one point are joined, and a chain whose ends meet closes — a trimmed lens or flower is one closed outline (the Frame layer's `d` is one closed subpath). Nothing moves; rules and fills carry; Cut and Dissolve unchanged; three-way meetings, guide/drawn mixes and line-tool line entities are not joined. 9 unit tests (8 fail without it), real-mouse checks on the pen page and in the Frame.

### The pen — round corners, chamfer, offset, repeat modes (stage 8) — LANDED 2026-09-27

Spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-26-pen-stages-4-8-design.md` (Stage 8), plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-27-pen-stage-8-corners-offset-repeat.md`; commits 9a63522eb … 08dfd2c98, final fixes aeb334470 + f2e98eaa6 + f84af46f6. On the pen page, in the Frame and in Shape Studio:

- **Round corner (F) and Chamfer (H):** press a corner and drag to size it, or click it and type a size + Enter; several corners share one size (tied Equal); too big previews red and can't be applied. The old corner stays as a grey guide dot tied to both sides, so rules on it still hold; side rules move to the side you see.
- **Offset (E):** drag from a path (or click and type; − flips the side) for a live parallel copy: lines to parallel lines, arcs on the same centre, sharp corners; smooth joins held on the arc's radial line; an offset that turns back or crosses itself previews red. New rules `offsetLine`, `offsetRadius`.
- **Repeat… panel** (in the side panel, replacing Properties while open): Radial (centre, copies, sweep — live), Linear (angle, Step or Span — live through `translatedFrom` to a dashed guide whose far end you can drag), Along a path (placed once). Preview only until Apply (one undo step); Esc/Cancel leaves the drawing as it was. Menu entries Offset…, Round corner…, Chamfer… after Repeat…; text-guide pens get corners, not Offset.
- Every new rule is followed by trim, Cut, Dissolve, merge, delete, Mirror, copy/paste, Clean up and fills.

**Proof.** ~1,455 unit tests across sketch/pen/cleanup/frame-pen/shape-pen; real-mouse `tests/pen-corners-offset-repeat.spec.ts` plus the other pen specs, 142/142, three hosts at 1280 and 1024. Previews ≤ 16 ms on a 150-piece connected drawing.

**Rulings beyond the plan.** A refused corner leaves the drawing byte-identical; smooth-join offset points use `collinear` (the plan's rule pair was singular); self-crossing offsets refused; repeat centre from the drawn geometry; a trimmed offset end re-aims only when the new end lies on the offset line; Repeat panel fields keep what you type when you leave them; Clean up refuses a solve that doesn't settle.

**Known limits.** Offsetting separate lines gives copies that don't join; paste and Along a path drop a corner's guide dot (a chamfer's two setbacks stop being tied); linear Apply takes 0.1–0.3 s at ~600 pieces; a rounded corner's radius chip covers its guide dot.

### The pen — fills (stage 7) — LANDED 2026-09-27

Spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-26-pen-stages-4-8-design.md` (Stage 7), plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-27-pen-stage-7-fills.md`; commits 0081a6279 … 38b4a0672, final fixes 65ade1e7f + 7c9b7e418. On the pen page, in the Frame and in Shape Studio:

- **Fill tool** (G, bucket): hover hatches the area under the pointer, click fills it, click a filled area to empty it. Areas are the faces the drawing's lines, arcs and circles make where they cross; guides don't bound them; ends that nearly meet (6 px at the first fill's zoom) are bridged, so the owner's trimmed flower fills petal by petal without joining.
- **Fills follow edits:** a drag carries them; a line across a filled area keeps both halves filled; removing a divider keeps one fill; opening a gap puts the fill to sleep with amber rings until it closes; trimming an area away drops its fill; Cut, Dissolve, Clean up, Repeat, Mirror, Flip, copy/paste (across tools) carry them; an overlapping copy fills its whole area and nothing outside it.
- **Output:** filled areas become one merged outline with true arcs. A Frame path layer gets `fillD` — the painter fills `fillD` and strokes `d` (editor, card, web export, bake, SVG export, mask "Use shape", long shadow, geometry effects); layers without fills paint byte-identically. A Drawn shape in Shape Studio with fills is its filled areas.

**Proof.** ~1,250 unit tests across sketch/pen/cleanup/frame-pen/geoshape plus 5,297 compositor/frame tests (byte-identity snapshot unchanged); real-mouse `tests/pen-fills.spec.ts` plus the other ten pen specs, 121/121, three hosts at 1280 and 1024.

**Rulings.** Holes nest (non-zero outlines); a fill is stored as a seed on an edge and settled against the last drawing inside every commit; a fill counts only on an area's outer edge; never fill or move a fill on a guess; one colour per drawing (the layer's fill, blue if it had none); Morph drops `fillD`; no Fill in text-guide pens or in the menu, wheel or Properties.

**Known limits.** Two exactly overlapping pieces don't split an area; Bézier pieces bound nothing; a Morph shows the closed-up outline while it runs. **Found, not stage 7:** dragging a point on a ~160-piece connected drawing stalls up to seconds a frame in the solver.

### The pen — right-click menu, action wheel, Properties (stage 6) — LANDED 2026-09-27

Spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-26-pen-stages-4-8-design.md` (Stage 6), plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-27-pen-stage-6-menus-properties.md`; commits 0e5333b79 … 42f0bfb89, final fix d47b364d2. On the pen page, in the Frame and in Shape Studio:

- **Right-click** opens a menu headed by what is selected ("2 points", "1 arc"): the rules the rules row offers, then Make guide (X), Flip (⇧H / ⇧V), Mirror…, Repeat…, Copy (⌘C), Copy as SVG, Paste (⌘V), Delete — each with its key; greyed items say why in their card. On empty space: Paste and Select all (⌘A). The ContextMenu key and ⇧F10 open it too.
- **Right-press and drag** opens an 8-slice action wheel (Zoah's layouts for points and for segments); release on a slice to run it, in the middle to cancel.
- **Properties** replaces the side panel while the pen is open: a point's X/Y, a line's length and angle (as seen on screen), an arc's radius (with a lock), length and sweep, a circle's radius (lock); the selection's rules by name ("Tangent — Line 2 · Arc 1"), hover lights both pieces, × removes, + adds any rule — refused in plain words ("Already true", "Conflicts with another rule").
- **Copy and paste inside the pen**, with the rules among the pieces, across the pen page, the Frame and Shape Studio at the same on-screen size.

**Proof.** 995 unit tests (pen-*, sketch-*, cleanup-*); real-mouse `tests/pen-menus.spec.ts` (20) plus the other nine pen specs, 112/112, all three hosts, 1280 and 1024 wide.

**Rulings.** One action registry feeds the menu, the wheel and Properties; the rules row is unchanged. Opening a menu does only cheap checks; the "Conflicts" check runs a small window solve only when a rule is hovered or picked, and an uncertain answer never refuses. Repeat and Mirror copy links stay hidden from Properties. A typed size is solved on a copy first and never leaves a rejected shape in the Frame. Piece names are numbered per kind in drawing order. While the pen is open the Frame's bottom tool row sits between its side panels.

**Known limits.** Stage 8 items (Offset, Round corner, Chamfer) are absent until built; screen readers don't hear the menu's keyboard highlight; on Windows/Linux ⇧F10 may also show the browser's menu; cross-host paste doesn't match a rotated target; a menu can float over a view the host re-fits itself.

### The pen — Clean up (stage 5) — LANDED 2026-09-27

Spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-26-pen-stages-4-8-design.md` (Stage 5), plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-26-pen-stage-5-clean-up.md`; commits 4fd5c9c64 … 1eac787b7, final fixes 53155595e, 4903cecf1, ccc8f34ac, c3c9bf2ea, e162be829. On the pen page, in the Frame and in Shape Studio:

- **Clean up** (button after Dissolve, or ⌥⇧C) makes a drawing feel right: it joins ends that nearly meet, pins points that nearly sit on a curve, smooths nearly smooth joints, squares nearly level/upright/parallel/square lines, gives nearly shared centres one centre, finds mirror pairs within a shape, evens nearly equal lengths, radii and spacing, and rounds sizes near a whole unit. It works on the selection, else the whole drawing.
- **Preview first:** the cleaned drawing shows solid over a faint ghost, one badge per fix ("Joined", "Tangent", "Square", "Same radius ×4"…); click a badge to switch that fix off or on; Gentle / Normal / Strong; Apply (Enter) is one undo step, Cancel (Esc) changes nothing. Arrow keys change the strength.
- **The owner's trimmed flower** (petals left as separate open pieces) joins into one closed loop, so the Frame fills its centre.

**Proof.** 809 unit tests (cleanup-*, pen-*, sketch-*) incl. a 148-piece connected drawing and a 37-rectangle grid; real-mouse `tests/pen-cleanup.spec.ts` plus the other eight pen specs, 89/89, at 1280 and 1024 wide, in all three hosts.

**Rulings.** Tolerances in screen px at the zoom Clean up opened at; always solved from the original drawing, cached per switch set; each fix solved in a small window first (two hops along pieces and rules), the whole connected part only if needed, a size nudge never; a fix is refused if it moves anything more than max(8 px, 10 %), flips or shrinks an arc, squeezes a line under 2 px, or adds nothing; Mirror pairs only inside one shape; an 800 ms budget per run ("Stopped early — select a part to clean up the rest"); a drawing whose own rules don't hold is refused; Enter on a focused Cancel cancels; the hosts skip Space-to-pan only inside the Clean up bar.

**Known limits.** Switching a fix off can surface new ones; badges are mouse-only; an Evenly spaced guide can join two shapes so a second run may pair them as mirrors; merges drop degenerate rules without counting them; shallow arcs can be refused by the centre movement cap.

### The pen — tangency done right (stage 4) — LANDED 2026-09-26

Spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-26-pen-stages-4-8-design.md`, plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-26-pen-stage-4-tangency.md`; commits 0ea65d6ed … 775281ade, final fixes cb3a5579d + 0883742ce. On the pen page, in the Frame and in Shape Studio:

- **Tangent on any two pieces, joined or not.** Select two segments (or a line or circle plus a segment) → Tangent. Joined pieces get the joint form; pieces apart get two new rules, `tangentLineArc` and `tangentArcs` (outside or inside, whichever is nearer when made). Two arcs also get **Equal** (same radius).
- **The bowing arc snaps tangent** to any nearby line, arc or circle, even one it isn't joined to: the ghost circle turns green, a T chip sits where it touches, a sparkle marks it, and letting go writes the rule. A line leaving an arc's end snaps onto its tangent and keeps the joint smooth.
- **Drag an arc's bow** in Select to resize it with its ends and rules holding; **⌘-drag** moves its centre. One undo step each.

**Proof.** Unit suites (pen-* and sketch-*, 694 tests) incl. Jacobian-vs-numeric checks for the new rules and 15 trim/cut/dissolve/merge cases with them; real-mouse `tests/pen-tangent.spec.ts` (5) plus sketch-draw, frame-pen, shape-pen, pen-trim, pen-tips, pen-snap, pen-weld — 75/75.

**Rulings.** `tangentLineArc` also takes a circle id; the joint's own tangency beats the bow snap; a typed radius skips it; a bow drag holds the ends first and frees them only if it must; the centre is warm-started at the circle through the ends and the pointer so a bow can cross its chord (the sweep flips); circles are not bow-dragged; arc Equal has no badge, like line Equal.

**Known limits.** Earlier segments of the path being drawn are not bow-snap targets; the Line tool starting at an arc's end doesn't snap tangent; trimming a circle into an arc drops its tangent rules (counted); joint tangency forms are not re-aimed when the arc is trimmed away from the joint (from stage 3); drop-to-join drops degenerate tangent rules without counting them.

### The pen — trim, tooltip cards, coincident (stages 1–3) — LANDED 2026-09-26

**Why.** The owner compared the pen with Zoah (formerly Opacity); we studied every Zoah video frame by frame, help.zoah.com and the CAD sketchers. This is stages 1–3 of an 8-stage programme (4 tangency, 5 clean up, 6 right-click menu / wheel / properties panel, 7 fills, 8 round corners / offset / repeat modes).

**What you can do.** Trim (T, scissors): hover a piece between crossings to see it, click to remove, press and sweep to remove several — one undo step; what's left ends at the crossing, pinned to the curve that cut it; dotted ghosts while Trim is armed. Cut (C) adds a point without moving the curve; Dissolve (D) merges two pieces that line up. Option-selected segments can be deleted. Every pen button has a tooltip card (name, key, caption; the nine tools have a small looping animation); tool keys V P B L O N T C D. Snapping onto path arcs, path line segments and midpoints, clamped to each curve's extent, with a ⊙ / midpoint / on-curve preview chip before the click. In Select, drop a dragged point onto a point to merge them or onto a curve to pin it (hold ⌘/Ctrl to just move); Coincident on two points merges them; a point plus an Option-clicked segment offers On curve / Midpoint. Joining the two ends of a near-full arc (over half a turn) closes it into a circle; joining the loose ends of two pieces rejoins them into one path (a loop closes). Joined points draw as small dots, loose ends as hollow squares, centres as hollow circles; labels stay on screen.

**Rules decided on the way** (from the ledger): Bézier segments are out of scope for crossings/trim/snapping; Cut adds no straightness rule (Dissolve heals where the pieces line up); a trim that moves an end drops length rules on that piece rather than moving the drawing; switching tools finishes a path you're drawing as an open line; a text guide always stays open; Coincident refuses a join that would collapse a piece; a Frame layer trimmed open gets a stroke.

**Proof.** Unit suites for crossings, trim, snapping, weld, point roles, chip clamp and tips (~630 tests across the pen/sketch files); real-mouse Playwright specs pen-trim, pen-tips, pen-snap, pen-weld — the full browser run went green on 2026-09-26 (70 tests across sketch-draw, frame-pen, shape-pen and the four new specs). It found one regression, fixed in 09e2a152a: wrapping the toolbar in the tooltip provider dropped the Frame's `pointer-events-auto`, so the Frame's pen buttons couldn't be clicked. frame-pen now clicks four tools with the real mouse.

**Known limits.** Bézier segments don't take part; retracing a sweep doesn't restore pieces; no Extend; a lone user point pinned only to a circle is removed when that circle or the piece holding it is trimmed; Trim's own-pin record can over-count "Removed N rules" after an undo; commit `a81e67cc5` carries a brush-engine message but holds trim fixes (a commit race).

Commits: `4a70c5cd2`, `64a39167e`, `87a6385b5`, `a71455531`, `a81e67cc5`, `0b37b678c`, `71d7b9a33`, `099b250db`, `17d25dfa3`, `b9d906e5b`, `694be537d`, `907325b75`, `a193e7ca2`, `9aeff5300`, `15dfa8b65`, `6b6e01e71`, `63d8f14df`; spec/plan `b1f4d9d46`.

### Frame brush tips — spray can, round, bristle — LANDED 2026-09-26 (Part 1 of "painting with shaders"; spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-26-frame-brush-tips-design.md`, plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-26-frame-brush-tips.md`, prototype `docs/superpowers/specs/assets/2026-09-26-shader-brush-prototype.html`; `a9777f232`..`dfaca8fe1`, non-contiguous — 10 tasks, subagent-driven, a review per task, a whole-feature review, its fix wave and a re-review)

The Frame brush's Paint mode now has three tips instead of round dabs: **Spray can** (fine speckle over a soft mist; holding still pools the paint, then it drips), **Round** (clean even stroke, a little overspray and grain at the edge), **Bristle** (thins a little when fast, tapers, dry-brush breakup). Quick controls sit in a bottom brush toolbar (tip, size, colour, eraser, Paint/Mask, Done); each tip's full settings are in the right panel with Julien's hand-tuned defaults and Reset, remembered per tip. Mask mode is unchanged.

A stroke saves its **movement** (pointer path + timing + tip settings + seed), not pixels — `TipStroke` in `lib/brushTips/record.ts`. Pure simulators (`spray.ts`, `round.ts`, `bristle.ts`) replay it deterministically in fixed Frame units (1080 per artboard width), so a stroke looks the same in the editor, node card, exports and embeds at any size. A small WebGL2 engine (`engine.ts`, Canvas2D fallback) turns that into a coverage shape; the brush layer's existing fill is poured in unchanged, so every fill (including shader fills) works — the base for Part 2 (materials) and Part 3 (the shader library, painted effects). Legacy brush strokes render byte-identically.

**Verified:** unit suites (determinism, live equals replay at any frame rate, bounds); real-mouse E2E on the running app (`tests/compositor-brush-tips.spec.ts`: one stroke = one undo step, drips then commit, reload redraws identical pixels, eraser rules, legacy draws; `tests/compositor-brush-tips-size.spec.ts`: same paint coverage at two render sizes).

**Known limits.** Undo while drips are still running first commits the dripping stroke. On a layer mixing old and new strokes, old ones always draw underneath. Spray and round strokes painted before the final fix re-grain once. Not yet tried with a real trackpad by Julien.

**Next.** Part 2: the seven curated materials (Round and Bristle strokes follow the line, the spray can stays fixed to the surface). Part 3: "More shaders" from the full library and painting effects onto the picture.

### Stroke fill follows the line — BUILT 2026-09-25 (spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-25-stroke-fill-follows-line.md`)

A Compositor stroke's patterned or gradient fill can now bend along the outline it paints instead of sitting still across it. Three new `StrokeInstance` fields carry it: `follow` (bool, on/off), `fade` ("across" | "along", an ombre paint's fade direction while following), and `fadeRepeats` (1..50, out-and-back fade cycles round the line when `fade` is "along"). Wired through the painter, the stroke inspector row, and the agent's `setStrokeProps`/`addStroke` patch (`STROKE_PROPS`, validated the same way as every other stroke key).

**Known limits.** Outer corners always render round while following. Acute star tips can show a small stripe jog. The per-frame cost of a following fill isn't measured or cached yet.

**Next.** A Motion-tab motion that runs the fill along the edge over time.

### Shared pen — Plan C (Shape Studio) — LANDED 2026-09-25 (spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-24-shared-pen-design.md`, plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-25-shared-pen-c-shape-studio.md`)

Shape Studio's Shape picker gains **Drawn**. Picking it opens the shared pen over the preview (its toolbar replaces the prompt dock while open); you draw one unit — arcs, curves, lines, circles, rules — and Enter makes it the layer's shape; "Edit the shape" reopens it. Everything Shape Studio does applies to the drawn unit: copies and arrangements (radial, grid, linear), overlaps, folds, fills, colour order, layers, background. The Size row scales it (it fits like a library shape: larger side = Size). An open drawing switches the layer to outline if it was filled. While the pen is open: the preview framing holds still, the other copies show faintly and update when you finish a gesture (the fold runs then, never mid-drag), Escape belongs to the pen (the studio doesn't close), the layer rail and the AI actions (Tune, Vary, Re-roll) are locked, and a right-click never places a point (that last fix is in the shared pen, so the Frame and the test page have it too).

Drawn is yours only: re-roll never picks it (the same seed rolls the same as before), Blend's second shape can't be Drawn, and the assistant can't choose it.

**Known limits.** A Collection row bound to Shape can still set Drawn on a layer with no drawing (renders empty); curves fit to their flattened outline (a hair under Size); Escape typed in a Placement field closes the studio and cancels the drawing; an open (unclosed) drawing is closed by the boolean fold — Unite/Subtract/Intersect gain a closing chord across the open ends — and by Blend, which always resamples to a closed loop; only Even-odd/Exclude keep it open.

**Proof.** Unit suites for the model, the fit, the pen geometry and the session; `tests/shape-pen.spec.ts` with the real mouse and keyboard (draw → radial ×12 fills 10 of 12 sectors; Escape keeps the studio open; right-click adds nothing; resize keeps the pen aligned).

Commits: `dae467c51`, `5b6b664e4`, `3f4708552`, `041922b91`, `d74b2e2f7`.

The shared-pen programme (Plans A, B, C) is complete.

### Shared pen — Plan B (the Frame) — LANDED 2026-09-25 (spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-24-shared-pen-design.md`, plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-25-shared-pen-b-frame.md`)

The Frame's toolbar pen now draws with the shared pen — arcs, Curve, lines, circles, points, rules — with its own two-row toolbar replacing the Frame's while it is open. A finished drawing becomes a path layer that remembers its drawing: closed shapes get a blue fill, open ones a blue outline. Double-clicking such a path reopens the pen on it, live on the layer, at any rotation, skew or scale — Enter saves one undo step, Esc puts it back, and opening the pen and leaving without an edit adds nothing to undo. Paths without a drawing (imported, library shapes, old ones) and corner-pinned paths still open the point editor.

The text tool's "Follow a path → Drawn path" now shares the same pen: "Draw a path" and "Edit the path" open it (open paths only — Select, Pen, Curve), the type re-lays as you draw and stays on the line you drew, and "Or use" copies a path's drawing too. While the pen is open, Escape, Delete and ⌘Z belong to the pen — the editor doesn't close and the layer isn't deleted — the Motion tab closes the pen, and the text's placement controls are disabled with "Finish the pen first". Typed values (Distance, Radius, Copies) are entered in a row in the toolbar, not a browser prompt. The old handles pen is deleted.

**Under the hood.** Drawings are stored at 100 units per local unit; the stored drawing and the outline always agree (the outline is derived from the drawing); re-centring happens only on save; and a text guide keeps its refit factor and moves the text so the type stays on the drawn line.

**Known limits.** With a radial cloner the pen's points sit at the layer's own centre, not on a copy. A cloner with per-copy rotation or scale saves without re-centring. A perfectly vertical guide shows at its unresized length. The viewing-size routes (double-click, Draw a path) have no browser test.

**Proof.** Unit suites for the pen, geometry and session; `tests/frame-pen.spec.ts` with real mouse and keyboard.

Commits: `006824859`, `9eb75250c`, `b652be159`, `2337a5fee`, `0f278efb2`, `c4dcef4e3`, `28c930250`, `038e52336`, `678c6d054`, `41b13fd34`, `861f0f5d3`, `0c8804076`, `2898e990d`, `dfdfc7202`, `dbe3786cf`, `b7bfdc135`, `058899be7`.

### AI in Sailor, stages 3–5 — results on the work, the studios, shader generation and My effects — BUILT overnight 2026-09-24/25 (subagent-driven: a review per task, a whole-stage review, one fix wave and re-reviews per stage; spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-23-ai-in-sailor-design.md` §3, §2.4, §7; plans `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-24-ai-in-sailor-stage3-results.md`, `…/2026-09-25-ai-in-sailor-stage4-studios.md`, `…/2026-09-25-ai-in-sailor-stage5-shader-gen.md`)

- **Stage 3, results on the work** (`58798f8fb`..`e7cc31c7f`, non-contiguous). A router (`/api/prompt-route`, Haiku, metered) sorts each request into one of nine kinds. Take strips sit above the prompt: the target node glows and previews the hovered take, Keep applies it, and nodes never move. Proposed nodes show on the canvas with a Proposed pill, and removals are only marked until Approve. Questions get an answer card, a kind with no worker says so plainly, and the working label quotes the request. `CanvasPromptBar` is retired. Stop, × and Keep interrupt the set's renders by prompt id.
- **Stage 4, the studios** (`de110c6e0`..`58f4390cf`, non-contiguous). The same prompt, docked above a small shared tool bar, is used in Shader, Gradient, Shape, Texture, Vector type, Space type, 3D, Frame and the template editor. Scattered controls moved into the bar; inspectors open with the thing, then light action rows, then dials. `VibeControlBar`, `AgentBar`, `AgentProgress`, `AgentProposal` and `TakeStrip` are retired, and a guard test fails if any screen brings back its own instruction prompt. Frame's Ask pill is gone. Frame and the template editor lock editing while a request runs, while its review runs and while a proposal is open (Approve or reject to keep editing), which restores the guarantee the old right-panel takeover gave. 3D mounts the prompt with no worker yet and answers plainly without a router call.
- **Stage 5, shader generation and My effects** (`c6c94f790`..`56fa9cef1`, non-contiguous). Remix…, New effect… and Make one ✦ write three new effects with Opus 5.5 (effort medium, a dedicated setting). Each take is checked and lands on its tile as soon as it passes, and hovering previews it on the target: a canvas shader node, the Shader studio's layer (or a new layer) or Frame's background. The target is read-only while a set is open. Keep saves to **My effects**, a per-user library (`/api/my-effects`, owner-scoped hosted, size-capped) that every shader picker lists first, through one shared gallery. The Recipe renames, shows where an effect came from, switches versions and removes. Each use stays on the version it was given. Projects keep a copy of the My effects they use, and exports inline them. The estimate (~$0.24–0.42 locally, ~48–88 credits hosted) shows before a request runs, from the same token prices the server charges with. The shader renderer recovers from a lost graphics card, and a take that caused the loss is dropped.
- **Owed.** A real-mouse pass on all three stages, including how the locked Frame and template editor look. One live router call (~1 credit). The two live studio checks: Gradient "warmer" and Frame "shorter headline". The stage 5 paid cost-and-latency measurement (spec §7.2). A `:3002` restart so Nitro registers `/api/my-effects`: until then the library list 404s there, and pickers show only effects kept in the session. Parked product calls are listed in the morning summary.

### AI in Sailor, stage 2 — one prompt on the canvas, and the node toolbar — BUILT 2026-09-24 (`9e8f9b39d`..`cacd0be87`, non-contiguous — 21 commits across 10 tasks + a final fix wave, subagent-driven, a review per task, a whole-stage review and its fix wave; spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-23-ai-in-sailor-design.md` §2, plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-24-ai-in-sailor-stage2-canvas.md`)

- **One prompt component** (`components/prompt/SailorPrompt.vue`): the canvas prompt now runs on it. The selection shows as a chip with the node's own title, 2–3 suggestions appear while focused, `/` and ⌘K focus it (never from behind a studio, Frame or Settings), and Esc leaves. The look is Sailor's existing AI look: the pastel ring, plus the glimm while working. Stop aborts the request, and a reply that arrives late is ignored; the same holds for reroll.
- **Explain is removed.** "What does this do?" in the prompt replaces it.
- **The node toolbar:** one registry of actions (`lib/canvas/nodeActions.ts`), grouped Edit / Develop, firing the same events the old menus did. A floating toolbar sits above the selected node and stays the same size at any zoom. It replaces the image and video Edit…/Develop… footers and the audio chips. Each row shows a ✦ for AI, a grey landing hint ("3 takes" / "adds a step"), and the price on paid actions. Several selected nodes get Run N · Group · Combine into Frame.
